warning[L-S05]: assignment to if can be replaced with select
   ΓöîΓöÇ addons/garage/Refuel/fn_refuelVehicleFromSources.sqf:61:24
   Γöé
61 Γöé         _fuelData set [if (A3A_hasAce) then {2} else {1}, 0];
   Γöé                        ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^ use select
   Γöé
   = note: the if and else blocks only return constant values
           select is faster in this case
   = try: [1, 2] select (A3A_hasAce)
